# HISTORY - the lab commits behind every moved file

> **Generated at the cut; the lab is not reachable from here.**
> This repo starts with no git history. Every file listed below came from the lab repo `Slava12233/RealEstateTeam` (private) at the tag `walkaround/cut-2026-11-01` (commit `f13dae5b`, 2026-11-01), at the same path.
> Under each file are the lab commits that touched it, newest first, exactly as this command printed them:
> `git log --format='%h %ad %s' --date=short walkaround/cut-2026-11-01 -- <path>`
> The hashes resolve only inside the lab. `PROVENANCE.json` holds each file's sha256; `scripts/cut/gen-history.mjs` wrote both files.

- **Files:** 283 in 75 folders
- **Lab commits:** 106 distinct, 771 file-commit pairs
